# The clustered interval floor of point TMLE and in-sample LTMLE

This study sets the clustered interval floor of each point-treatment fit family and of in-sample
`LTMLE`: the smallest positive-mass cluster count from which the interval on $t_{J-2}$ carries no
experimental label ([few clusters](../inference.md#few-clusters)). It measures every count of a
ladder below the old floors, 10 for a point fit and 20 for `LTMLE`, and reads each family's floor
by the rule declared before the run. On the same draws it compares the shipped reference (A) with
Wang et al.'s inflated variance (B) and the delete-one-cluster jackknife (C).

No other implementation computes these intervals at these counts, so the study has no comparator.
The publication policy is `reporting`. Every property cell reads one contrast: the ATE for the point
fits, and always against never for `LTMLE`. `CLUSTER_INTERVAL_FLOORS` ships each measured floor for
that contrast alone, and every other estimate keeps 10 or 20. 74 property cells and one primary row
are red. The [red-cell ledger](red-cells.md) holds all 74 cells and the primary row under
`X54-cluster-floor`, one of them as `superseded (study defect)`.

| part | what it is | status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| primary | in-sample `LTMLE` at 10 clusters of `unequal_informative`, 1,000 replications: the two arm means and always against never | the accuracy table below. The arm mean `ey_regimen[never]` is red, below the arm-mean floor of 20 |
| property family `few_cluster_reference` | each fit at each count of its ladder on two size laws, 4,000 draws or more per cell: the shipped interval (A), its IID control and reported references | the tables below give the verdicts |
| property families `few_cluster_candidate` and `few_cluster_candidate_pooled` | (B) and (C) against a copy of (A) on the first 2,000 draws of each cell, per cell and pooled | the part (c) decision below |
| comparator runs | none | no other implementation computes these intervals |

## What was fitted

The two size laws are those of `clustered-few-cluster-tmle`. `equal10` holds clusters of 10 rows
with an arm-by-latent coefficient of 8, and `unequal_informative` draws sizes uniformly on 2 to 18,
with the size informative of the outcome. The truths are exact. The point fits run at 4 to 10, 15,
20, 30 and 39 clusters, and in-sample `LTMLE` at 4 to 20, 30 and 39.

| fit | construction | floor family |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `tmle_in_sample_known`, `tmle_in_sample_estimated` | `TMLE(cross_fit=False)`, the propensity declared known or fitted by a binomial main-terms GLM | in-sample `TMLE` |
| `tmle_stacked_known`, `tmle_stacked_estimated` | `cross_fit=True`, $V = \min(5, J)$ whole-cluster folds | stacked `TMLE` |
| `tmle_fold_evaluated_known`, `tmle_fold_evaluated_estimated` | `cv_evaluation=True`, $V = \min(5, \lfloor J/2 \rfloor)$, `equal10` only | fold-evaluated `TMLE` |
| `drtmle_known` | cross-fitted `DRTMLE`, foldwise reduced regressions, the propensity declared known | declared-mechanism `DRTMLE` |
| `tmle_stacked_d5_known`, `tmle_stacked_d5_estimated` | the stacked fit with three noise covariates added, `unequal_informative` only | stacked `TMLE`, reported |
| `ltmle_in_sample` | in-sample `LTMLE` on one node, a quasibinomial GLM and the law's exact propensity | in-sample `LTMLE` |

Every `TMLE` outcome regression is an unpenalized logistic regression in the fit's covariates.
The arms, laws, ladders and seeds are in `tests/studies/few_cluster_floor_tmle.py`, and
`tests/unit/test_few_cluster_floor_design.py` recomputes them.

## Accuracy against known truth

<!-- generated: accuracy -->
| law | estimand | what was tested | implementation | bias (99% interval) | coverage | SE ratio | result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 10 clusters of size uniform on 2 to 18 with the size in the outcome, an in-sample LTMLE on one node with parametric learners, the shipped report | `ate_regimen[always vs never]` | difference in mean outcome between the plans "treat" against "do not treat" | `cleverly` | 0.0072 to 0.0371 | 0.9310 | 0.9083 | pass |
| 10 clusters of size uniform on 2 to 18 with the size in the outcome, an in-sample LTMLE on one node with parametric learners, the shipped report | `ey_regimen[always]` | mean outcome under the plan treat | `cleverly` | 0.0068 to 0.0265 | 0.9310 | 0.9208 | pass |
| 10 clusters of size uniform on 2 to 18 with the size in the outcome, an in-sample LTMLE on one node with parametric learners, the shipped report | `ey_regimen[never]` | mean outcome under the plan do not treat | `cleverly` | -0.0140 to 0.0030 | 0.9140 | 0.8874 | **fail** |
<!-- /generated -->

## Agreement with the canonical implementation

None claimed. No canonical implementation is compared, because no other implementation computes
these intervals at these counts. [0/0 external comparisons; the row rests on the other two columns]

| --- | --- | --- |
| coverage floor | `t_reference` | the exact 99% lower end of coverage over every requested draw, a withheld or lost draw a miss, at least 0.90 |

| paired positive se ratio at most the sanity ceiling | `iid_t_control` | the upper end of the positive's shown-only SE ratio at most 1.20, so an inflated cluster SE cannot pass the control |

## The declared route

The design test `tests/unit/test_few_cluster_floor_design.py` reads each family's floor from the
committed summary and checks it against the shipped floor. A family's floor is the smallest count from
which every count of its ladder passes.

| family | route | floor of the measured contrast | red cells, owner and reading |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| in-sample `TMLE` | lowered | 6 | `t_reference` at 4 and 5 on `equal10`, both mechanisms: `X54-cluster-floor`, `below floor (finite-sample)` |
| stacked `TMLE` | redeclaration | 10 | `t_reference` at 4, and to 6 in the five-covariate estimated configuration; `iid_t_control` to 6, and to 9 in that configuration (`control power`); that configuration's control at 10: `X54-cluster-floor`, `superseded (study defect)` |
| fold-evaluated `TMLE` | lowered | 8 | `t_reference` at 4 and 5 (one `below floor (availability)`); `iid_t_control` to 7 (`control power`) |
| declared-mechanism `DRTMLE` | lowered | 8 | `t_reference` at 4 on `unequal_informative`; `iid_t_control` to 7 (`control power`) |
| in-sample `LTMLE` | lowered | 6 | `t_reference` at 4 and 5 on `equal10` and 4 on `unequal_informative` |

Every cell of the family reads one contrast, so the floor certifies that contrast and no other
estimate. The arm means, the ATT, the ATC, the ratios and any other contrast keep the old floor,
10 for a point fit and 20 for `LTMLE`, and take `few_cluster_estimand_plugin` below it. The one
arm-mean measurement below an old floor is the primary's: in-sample `LTMLE` at 10 clusters, where
`ey_regimen[never]` covers 0.914 with a 99% lower end of 0.889 and SE ratio 0.887. The contrast
covers 0.931 on the same draws. This rule and the primary's reading were added after the run, as
corrections of two omissions in the declaration. Neither moves a gate, a threshold, a replication
count or a verdict.

**The stacked floor stays 10, pending a redeclaration.** The two-covariate stacked cells alone
would give 7. The five-covariate estimated configuration at 10 clusters reads SE ratio 1.158
[1.062, 1.390]: its cluster SE is conservative, its coverage holds (0.956, lower end 0.949), but
the positive's SE-ratio upper end exceeds 1.20, so its IID control cannot be licensed. No committed
cell measures a five-covariate stacked fit, so the declared route sends the family to a
redeclaration ([X54](../../roadmap.md#x54-small-sample-evidence-for-clustered-intervals-and-boundary-nodes)
part (j)). The same configuration reads 1.048 at 15 and 20 clusters.

## Part (c): the reference and the candidates

(A) is the shipped $t_{J-2}$ interval. (B) inflates the variance by $\frac{J}{J-p}$ and reads
$t_{J-p}$. (C) is the delete-one-cluster jackknife. The decision counts, per family, the cells where
each construction is calibrated, and adopts (B) only if it wins every compared family.

| family | calibrated cells (A, B, C) | winner | jackknife (C) | as shipped |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| in-sample `TMLE` | 4, 9, - | (B) | not admissible: the paired coverage gain at `equal10`, 6 clusters, has lower end -0.07 against -0.03 | refused, roadmap F28 |
| stacked `TMLE` | 5, 2, 5 | (A) | admissible from 10 | refused while the family is redeclared (a declared departure) |
| in-sample `LTMLE` | 9, 17, 17 | (B) | admissible from 6 | an option under the experimental label at every count, below 6 too, with no support row (a declared departure) |

(B) loses stacked `TMLE`, so the package keeps (A) for every family.
[`few-cluster-floor-cross-fitted-ltmle`](few-cluster-floor-cross-fitted-ltmle.md) reads the
fourth compared family, where (B) wins too.

Two statements were fixed after the run and before any redeclaration. First, the stacked part (c)
result stands as measured, with (A) the winner, and the redeclaration covers only the stacked
floor and its control reading. So a later stacked comparison cannot make (B) win every family and
move the floors registered here. Second, the declaration ships an admissible (C) as an option.
The stacked jackknife is withheld anyway, as a declared departure: its admissibility was read
against the stacked (A) on the same configurations, the five-covariate one included, and that (A)
is under redeclaration. The departure withholds a construction and widens no claim, and the
redeclaration decides the stacked jackknife.

The `LTMLE` jackknife departs from the declaration too. The declared rule would have listed an
admissible (C) in a support row from its admissible count to 39 clusters. No support row lists
it, so every jackknife interval carries the experimental label. It is offered at every count the
fit accepts, below the admissible count of 6 too, where no cell found it non-inferior to (A). The
departure withholds a label and widens no claim.

## Reported readings

These readings carry no verdict.

- The five-covariate estimated stacked fit loses 805 of its 6,000 draws at 4 clusters (13.4%) to
  a singular treatment GLM, then 181 at 5, 53 at 6, 16 at 7 and 1 at 8. The pilot read 11.8%.
- Both 10-cluster reds the pilot predicted passed. In-sample known `TMLE` on `equal10` covers
  0.934, against 0.930 in the committed study. The fold-evaluated controls at 10 clusters read
  paired ratios 0.761 (known) and 0.772 (estimated), against the committed 0.758. The pilot's
  0.804 was a shared-draw event, so the declared comparison of a fold-evaluated red at 10
  clusters was not needed.
- The `normal_reference` and `t_j_minus_1_reference` arms, and (B) on every draw, read the same
  fits on other references, and are reported only.

## Red-cell route

The study is registered under `reporting`. The red-cell readings and every family's route were
declared before the run in `tests/canonical/few_cluster_floor_tmle/regenerate.py`. Each red row
and its owner is computed from the committed results by `red_owners` and `primary_owners` in
`tests/studies/few_cluster_floor.py`. A red that no reading holds fails the design test, unless
`SUPERSEDED_REDS` names it: the five-covariate estimated control at 10 clusters, which
`few-cluster-floor-stacked` re-measures.

## Limitations

- **One measured contrast.** Every property cell reads the ATE or always against never. The arm
  means and every other estimate keep the old floors, and the one arm-mean measurement below them
  fails.
- **Parametric learners.** Every fit uses unpenalized logistic regressions or a quasibinomial GLM.
  Wang et al. (2024), Remark 3, caution against flexible learners at about 20 clusters.
  [`few-cluster-adaptive-learners`](few-cluster-adaptive-learners.md) measures the default learner
  library at 10, 20 and 40 clusters.
- **Two size laws and binary outcomes.** `equal10` and `unequal_informative` only, a binary
  treatment, no weights, strata or missing outcomes; the fold-evaluated fit on equal sizes only.
- **One-node in-sample `LTMLE`.** The in-sample `LTMLE` cells fit one binary treatment node
  without censoring, so the floor of 6 holds on that design alone and any other keeps 20.
- **Stacked floor under redeclaration.** The stacked floor and its jackknife wait for X54 part (j).
- **No comparator.** No other implementation computes these intervals at these counts.
